# Matching insights

Use `python3 tools/match.py insight add "..." --category compiler` to record
short, reusable findings for every matching worker. Record an observation only
after reproducing it in the target or a controlled probe; include the relevant
compiler option, construct, and outcome.

- [compiler] CodeWarrior PS2 3.0 (2001-11-26) emits the target's `daddu` zero
  idiom and `sd`/`ld` return-address save for `fn_pstAllocMind`; MWCC 2.4 Build
  0017 instead emits `pextlb` and `sq`/`lq`.
- [compiler] Current probes make `-opt level=3` and `-opt level=4`
  codegen-equivalent. Use level 3 as the baseline unless a verified function
  disproves it.
- [source-layout] MWCC line-table offsets can reveal source formatting. The
  matched `fn_vInterruptMenu2D` required braced `if`/`else` bodies and its
  final clamp on one source line to reproduce the target line-offset pattern.

- [tooling] Cached match checks take about 0.3 seconds when the source body is unchanged; run index --refresh only after extraction changes ownership comments. (2026-09-06)

- [tooling] Difficulty triage uses target code size, branch/call count, memory
  pressure, FP/VU instructions, debug-line complexity, and C++ status. It
  selects the least-cost model bucket; an early mismatch should still trigger
  escalation rather than forcing an unsuitable worker to persist. (2026-09-06)

- [debug-format] MWCC linked .debug function records may omit unused leading parameters: Micros null callbacks record only their used pointer 0x83/0x63 descriptors, while _tdstAllRetObjectSound's function-pointer types preserve the full signatures and target code uses the resulting a2/a3/t0 registers. Treat such extracted parameter lists as incomplete when callback type or C++ mangling proves greater arity. (2026-09-06)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 recognizes #pragma schedule off/reset. On fn_ucFindPlaceInActionTable it restores the target's 100-byte size and unscheduled outer/final return slots, but changes loop-init and increment ordering, proving the target was not compiled with scheduling disabled for the whole function. (2026-09-06)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0: assigning a masked ternary expression to an unsigned int local and returning it from an unsigned-char function preserves the final andi 0xff conversion; equivalent early returns let range analysis remove that mask. (2026-09-06)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 emits a post-call andi 0xff when an undeclared callee is implicitly assumed to return int; declaring its unsigned-char return type removes that conversion. (2026-09-06)

- [compiler] For GLI_vResetTableOfActiveLightsForChar, leaving memset without an explicit prototype reproduced the target tail-call exactly (24/24); declaring memset as void or void* changed argument evaluation/register scheduling and emitted 28 bytes. MWCC's implicit builtin/library handling is significant for memset wrappers. (2026-09-06)

- [debug-format] Keyword wrappers required correcting tduGetSetParam_ from generated struct to union (all members at offset 0) plus an omitted leading ABI parameter; fn_p_stNoVMTKeyWord then matched 20/20 with p_stTree in a1 and p_stValue in a2. (2026-09-06)

- [relocation] In HMU_Sync.c, the target encodes the shared HMU_g_bHMU_CS_Created relocation as gp-0x6614 in HMU_fn_bIsInitialized but gp-0x6610 in matched HMU_fn_vEnterCriticalSection. A single natural source declaration can match the latter but not both; treat isolated accessor failures as target relocation/data-layout evidence. (2026-09-06)

- [debug-format] fn_ucGetDsgVarBoolean is a three-argument ABI despite the recovered two-argument declaration; target loads byte from a0, writes output through a2, and tags it E_vt_Integer (6), not E_vt_Boolean (1). This exact natural-C signature/tag change produced a 24/24 MATCH. (2026-09-06)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 level 3 only runs its loop unroller in speed mode: #pragma optimize_for_size off changed a signed-int 9-trip struct-array accumulation from an 84-byte rolled loop to a 320-byte factor-8 main loop plus scalar remainder; the compiler help independently states that loop unrolling requires -opt speed. (2026-09-06)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0: local #pragma optimize_for_size off changed repeated 0.001f and 0.7f constants from small-data literal loads into target-shaped lui/ori/mtc1 immediates in TXT_vAfficheSpecialWithTransparency; it did not change float-to-unsigned-char lowering through fptoui. (2026-09-07)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 recognizes scoped #pragma fast_fptosi on/reset: under the accepted -opt level=3 baseline it changes natural signed float-to-int casts from fptosi calls into hardware cvt.w.s/mfc1, while unsigned int/char casts still call fptoui. (2026-09-07)

- [debug-format] GLD_bWriteToViewportFinished's recovered one-parameter declaration omitted a leading ABI parameter: the target consumes hVP from , and adding an unused leading void* plus the natural hVP>>4/negative correction and (short) index cast produced the exact 64-byte match. Treat single-parameter MWCC records with unexpected  use as possible omitted leading parameters. (2026-09-13)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 level 3: #pragma optimize_for_size off enables strength-reduced byte-offset induction for variable-bound loops over 80-byte struct arrays; default size mode recalculates index*80 and saves one fewer callee-saved register. (2026-09-13)

- [debug-format] Five trivial procedure callbacks in Procedur.c had extracted one-parameter signatures but target ABI returned ; adding a second natural pointer parameter and returning it reproduced jr/daddu exactly, confirming omitted leading callback parameters in debug extraction. (2026-09-13)

- [source-layout] In u_drawHier.c, SDC_VertexArrays_.pArrayOfVertexArrays is one pointer at +0xc, not a four-element pointer array: the following debug-field offsets place FrameDeformed at +0x1c; correcting the generated declaration reproduced GLI_vSetObjectDeformed's FrameDeformed store. (2026-09-13)

- [abi] SAI_Pub b-prefixed wrappers are actually int-returning and their flag parameters are int, despite debug-recovered unsigned char/unsigned short types. Unsigned narrowing inserted andi/mask instructions and stack frames; changing wrappers to int reproduced all 8 target tail-call bodies exactly. (2026-09-13)

- [debug-format] AiCondSound.c required explicit cross-TU declarations for fn_p_stEvalTree and SND_fn_bGetStereoSound; extractor emitted an undefined p_SuperObjPerso use in the two-parameter fn_p_stIsInStereoMode body. Adding natural prototypes and removing the stale cast restored TU compilation. (2026-09-13)

- [source-layout] AiCondSound fn_p_stIsEventValid matches when the output parameter is backed by int st_Param[4] (not a scalar or recovered tdstGetSetParam_ object); MWCC then places the temporary at sp+0x20 and emits the target addiu/movz boolean sequence. (2026-09-14)

- [abi] Two recovered wrappers with debug unsigned-char return records matched only when declared int: fn_bDoReflexPerso (56/56) and fn_ucWaitEndOfAction (44/44). MWCC target returns full 32/64-bit expression without andi; the debug return type is not sufficient to reconstruct source ABI. (2026-09-14)

- [debug-format] CollSet no-collision setters all require an int condition parameter for MWCC ABI: changing the extractor-generated unsigned char parameter to int produced exact 44-byte matches for the ZDE projectile, other sectors, main character, secondary character, projectile, and map setters. The target branches directly on a1; unsigned-char candidates emitted an extra andi/mask. (2026-09-14)

- [debug-format] Extractor aggregate sizing must treat long and unsigned long as 8-byte MWCC PS2 types; treating them as 4 bytes inserts false internal padding and shifts enclosing fields (confirmed by _stTimerCount and engine-layout matches). (2026-09-14)

- [debug-format] LNK_fn_p_SetLag raw debug signature decoded only one tdstNodeInterpret_* parameter, but target ABI returns ; adding the omitted leading HIE_tdstSuperObject_* parameter and returning the second argument reproduces the exact 8-byte thunk. Treat similar one-parameter callback wrappers as possible signature-gap cases and validate register usage. (2026-09-14)

- [compiler] SDLRefs printf probe: under MWCC PS2 3.0 O3, a C++ member method calling extern "C" printf emits a 0x20-byte stack-frame jal even with void/non-variadic declarations; target SDLRefs methods are 12-byte lui/j tail thunks. Recovered target literals are @820="Dummy interpol reference called !!\\n" and @823="Dummy reference called !!\\n". This points to a project macro/special declaration or compiler-context difference, not unknown string contents. (2026-09-14)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 preserves independent byte-offset and element-index induction variables in a variable-bound handle scan; accessing the handle through (char*)base + Offset and incrementing Offset before i reproduces the target's addiu 0x10/addiu 1 loop order. (2026-09-15)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 baseline does not recognize __builtin_sqrt in recovered C: both PS2SQRTF and INT_fn_xDistancePointToLine lower it through fptodp plus an unresolved __builtin_sqrt call, while retail targets use inline sqrt.s. Exact matches need the original compiler-specific intrinsic/macro context. (2026-09-23)

- [source-layout] MECTools.c DNM_stDynamicsBaseBlock needs extractor padding +0x78..0x7f before m_stPreviousMatrix and +0x16c..0x16f before the advanced block; target accesses then align at previous=0x80, current=0xe0, advanced=0x170, while all three recorded MECTools matches remain MATCH. (2026-09-23)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 baseline lowers a plain float fabsf declaration to jal calls, while MAT_fn_bAreEquivalentWithEps and both MAT compose targets use inline abs.s; natural conditional absolute-value forms instead emit branch-heavy 1620-1796-byte code. Exact abs.s reconstruction needs the original project macro/header compiler context. (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] Exact MWCC PS2 3.0 probe: plain fabsf, __builtin_fabsf, __fabs, __fabsf, C++ abs(float), conditional sign tests, and union bit-mask forms do not emit abs.s under the project profile; ordinary fabs forms remain relocatable calls. This points to a missing project/SDK macro or inline-header construct, not an O3/O4 flag difference. (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] MWCCPS2 3.0 contains internal intrinsic __I_abs_s with signature __I_abs_s(long destination_reg, long source_reg); immediate register IDs are required and it emits abs.s directly (verified __I_abs_s(0,1) -> abs.s f0,f1). Natural abs_s(x), __abs_s(x), and __builtin_abs_s(x) are treated as external calls/conversions, so a project macro would need to wrap the internal register-form intrinsic or use an SDK inline helper. (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] Compiler binary intrinsic table exposes __I_abs_s with signature =D,S and opcode abs.s; direct calls require immediate physical FPU register IDs and emit the instruction, but do not create a C float value or influence MWCC register allocation. In a natural  probe, MWCC kept x in f1/f12, proving the intrinsic cannot be wrapped generically without the original register-binding/assembly convention. (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] Probe x=x-y; __I_abs_s(0,12); return x: MWCC kept x in f1/f12, so the direct intrinsic did not bind the C value to the requested source register. It cannot be wrapped generically without the original register-binding or assembly convention. (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 accepts a function-level asm definition such as `asm float f(float x) { abs.s $f0, $f12 }` and an inline `asm volatile { abs.s $f0, $f0 }` block; the former emits a standalone 4-byte wrapper using ABI f12->f0, while the latter preserves statement order but MWCC treats registers as unconstrained (it may allocate the C temporary in f1). This confirms the missing abs.s path likely came from an SDK asm helper/macro, not a normal fabs function or __I_abs_s wrapper; do not use inline asm in canonical sources. (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 inline asm accepts numeric FPU names (`$f0`, `$f12`) in asm function bodies, but SDK-style aliases such as `$fv0` and `$fa0` are rejected as undefined identifiers; any original wrapper must use numeric registers or a macro layer that expands aliases before MWCC parsing. (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 symbolic inline-asm operands bind C float lvalues to allocated FPU registers: `float d=x-y; asm { abs.s d,d }` emits exact `sub.s f0,f12,f13; abs.s f0,f0; c.lt.s f0,f14`. A header macro `#define MTH_ABS_IN_PLACE(v) asm { abs.s v,v }` preserves the same sequence for pointer loads and chained comparisons; plain and volatile asm are byte-identical, while asm functions never inline even with `-inline all`. (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0: to preserve chained-&& lowering around a statement-form symbolic abs helper, keep comparisons in an int temporary and gate later blocks with `if (test = !!test)`. This emits the target `sltu test,zero,test; beqz test` pair; assigning to an unsigned-char result only at case exit emits one final `andi`, reproducing MAT_fn_bAreEquivalentWithEps instruction-for-instruction. (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 lowers a 12-byte float-vector struct assignment through lwc1/swc1 plus a local-address temporary, but a union view with an unsigned-int[3] word copy emits direct lw/sw to stack slots. In CAM_fn_vComputeCameraSystemAxis this changed a 1020-byte candidate to the target 1016-byte size and reproduced the retail copy core exactly. (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 symbolic FP inline-asm operands accept scalar C float lvalues but reject aggregate-member expressions such as vector.x directly with 'illegal operand'; assign the component to a scalar temporary before applying recovered mula.s/madda.s/madd.s, sqrt.s, or abs.s helper macros. (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 C++ exceptions default to on under the baseline invocation and suppress level-3 tail-call optimization: adding `#pragma exceptions off` changed usnd_memcmp/usnd_memcpy/usnd_memset from 28-byte jal frames to exact 8-byte j thunks, and usnd_vDeleteCritSect from 40 bytes to the exact 20-byte tail jump. Use this only where retail C++ wrappers prove exception-free tail calls. (2026-09-24)

- [debug-format] MWCC PS2 treats unsigned long as 8 bytes; an extractor-inserted 4-byte pad between consecutive unsigned long fields in _stTimerCount made each timer record 8 bytes too large and shifted later tdstEngineStructure fields. Removing that pad fixed the stCineManager relocation by 0x10 in CineDef.c. (2026-09-24)

- [debug-format] GLI_vSetDynShadowParam's recovered four-parameter declaration was ABI-incomplete: target callers populate a1 with an object pointer, f12 with length, a2 with level, and a3 with close-state. Restoring an ignored void* second parameter yields the exact 20-byte target (a0/f12/a2/a3 stores). Use caller register setup to audit debug signatures when hard-float parameters appear to compress GPR numbering. (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 function-local #pragma optimization_level 1 preserves simple load/store optimization but leaves jr delay slots as nop (exact for SVG_fn_bGetActiveVms and SVG_fn_vSetMC0AsChanged); level 0 additionally spills and reloads an int parameter at stack offset 0 (exact for SVG_fn_SetAsCurrentMemoryCard). (2026-09-24)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 O3 folds pointer postincrement followed by returning that pointer into addiu on v0, but an explicit byte-pointer reassignment keeps the addiu in the parameter register and emits the return-register move in the jr delay slot. This exact distinction matched 18 Intell evaluator callbacks. (2026-09-24)

- [debug-format] MWCC debug definition records omit unreferenced parameters, but a same-TU declaration record keeps the full prototype. AGO2_CreateCylinder reads its part pointer from a1 and floats from f12/f13 while its definition records only (AGO2_Part_*, float, float); the declaration has the full 16-parameter callback type. tools/extract_debug.py now completes such definitions (26 in the retail ELF, marked with a "Signature completed" comment) when the recovered types are an in-order subsequence. Files extracted earlier still show the short form: check signature evidence before trusting a short parameter list. (2026-09-26)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2: field-by-field float copies written through int lvalues (*(int*)&d->xX = *(int*)&s->xX, xY, xZ) compile to interleaved lw/sw via a GPR, which is how the retail code copies MTH vectors (e.g. set-translation in MEC_fn_vGroundObstacleManagement, with lui 0x3F80 for xW=1.0f scheduled after the first lw). Plain struct assignment, V3 casts, or float field copies all use lwc1/swc1 instead. (2026-09-26)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2: a comparison cast to unsigned char, if ((unsigned char)(p->e != 99)), is materialized as xori/sltu/beqz; plain comparisons, ?1:0, int casts and != 0 wrappers branch directly (beq/bne). Retail MEC code uses this for boolean-returning macros (m_xCollidedEntity != 99, m_hPlatform != 0). (2026-09-26)

- [relocation] The extractor types some pointer globals as int (g_a_stObstacle in MECBhv.c, g_pPS2glApiTable in PS2GL_ApiTable.c). Symptom: source written as (T*)&g_x, which compiles to addiu reg,gp,off while the target loads the pointer with lw reg,off(gp); relocations still match because the symbol is the same. Check the size-4 symbol and the target lw before trusting &g_x casts. (2026-09-26)

- [debug-format] When histogramming target opcodes, construct rabbitizer.Instruction with category=rabbitizer.InstrCategory.R5900; the default MIPS decoder shows mula.s/madda.s/madd.s (used by the MTH_*_TO asm macros, which do match the retail code) as .word. (2026-09-26)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0: assigning through a pointer to a plain struct { float f[4]; } __attribute__((aligned(16))) (16 bytes, 16-byte aligned) compiles the whole-object copy as a single lq/sq quadword pair. The same copy through a union { unsigned long long u64[2]; float f[4]; } (align 8) instead compiles as two ld/sd pairs (8 bytes larger). Confirmed on impPS2glTexCoord4fv/impPS2glMultiTexCoord4fvARB in PS2GL_DataInput.c. (2026-09-26)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2: abs.s inside a lazy && chain (retail MEC null-vector-with-epsilon tests: first compare raw beqz, second sltu, third andi 0xFF) is reproduced by a C inline wrapper around statement asm, inline float MTH_xAbs(float x) { float r; asm { abs.s r, x } return r; }, but only when compiled with -inline on (any mode); function-level asm float f(register float) helpers are never inlined, and no pragma (dont_inline off, inline_depth, always_inline) overrides the command-line -inline off. Re-verifying all 1,150 recorded matches with -inline on in place of -inline off gave 1,150/1,150 MATCH, so the baseline flag is not constrained by any current match. (2026-09-26)

- [source-layout] PS2GL_ApiTable.c wrappers are hand-written asm functions, not C: one line-map entry per instruction (lw, lw, jr; delay-slot nop on the jr line), the function pointer in v1 (all 18 compiler-generated lw/lw/jr dispatches elsewhere in the ELF reuse one register), (void) debug records, and a pure tail jump even for PS2glTexImage2D with a stack argument. asm void PS2glX(void) { lw v0, g_pPS2glApiTable; lw v1, tagPS2glApiTable.pfn_PS2glX(v0); jr v1; nop } matches all 136 byte-for-byte with the line map. A per-instruction line map with non-MWCC register choices is the signal to look for hand-written asm. (2026-09-26)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 C++ with exceptions enabled (the default) never emits tail calls: return f(x) becomes a framed jal. The retail C++ (usnd file wrappers, VUSprites, SND, PS2MC) uses bare j tail calls, reproduced by -Cpp_exceptions off or #pragma exceptions off. Adding -Cpp_exceptions off to the baseline kept all 1,292 recorded matches and matched 27 more C++ functions with no source change, so the baseline now includes it. (2026-09-26)

- [compiler] The retail ELF contains 1,260 inline cvt.w.s float-to-int conversions and no calls to fptosi/fptoui (only fptodp/dptofp for doubles), so the whole game was built with fast float-to-int. The baseline now passes -pragma "fast_fptosi on"; this kept all 1,399 recorded matches and matched 3 more with no source change. (2026-09-26)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 size optimization materializes 32767.0f through integer mtc1/cvt.s.w, adding four bytes to short vertex wrappers. Scoped optimize_for_size off/reset uses direct lui/ori/mtc1 float bits and verifies MATCH for both recPS2glVertex2s (56 bytes) and recPS2glVertex2sv (64 bytes); no baseline change. (2026-09-26)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 with baseline -inline on still leaves ordinary recorder calls uninlined. Defining scalar recorder before vector wrapper and scoping pragma auto_inline on/reset over both produces exact 72-byte recPS2glRasterPos2sv/iv/fv and recPS2glEvalCoord2fv matches, including line offsets, while preserving all four scalar MATCH symbols. Explicit inline also matches wrappers but omits the standalone scalar symbol. (2026-09-27)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 playback wrappers: when extraction represents a pointer field as int, (void*)p->pixels evaluates the conversion first and changes load order (plyPS2glTexImage1D 36/40 bytes). Reading pointer storage as *(void**)&p->pixels preserves the declaration and yields exact 40-byte MATCH with line offsets. Independently reproduced with *(float**)&p->points in plyPS2glMap2f (48-byte MATCH), using callback signature from tagPS2glApiTable. (2026-09-27)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 auto-inlining preserves callee local-declaration order in register coloring even when standalone callee bytes are unchanged. Declaring tagGLlistnode* pNode before anon_N* pParam in recPS2glRasterPos2f/3f/4f fixes their double scalar callers s0/s1 allocation and prologue order, yielding MATCH at 104/128/152 bytes. Independently verifies all nine float, float-vector, and double-vector callees/callers unchanged. Initial isolated experiment certified by tools/permute.py verify. (2026-09-27)

- [compiler] For C wrappers with an explicit if/else return, scoped #pragma schedule off/reset can reproduce target NOP delay slots and a shared unscheduled epilogue without assembly. MC_MemoryCardChanged: early-return C produced 44/60 bytes; explicit if/else 52/60; same if/else with schedule off MATCH 60/60, two named calls unchanged. Distinguish this pattern from loop residuals where schedule off disrupts otherwise-correct instruction order. (2026-09-27)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0: returning a 64-bit division expression directly from an unsigned-int function emits dsll32/dsra32 narrowing, but assigning the quotient back to the recovered unsigned-long local and returning that local removes both instructions. usnd_uiRand improves 80/72 to MATCH 72/72 with rand, __muldi3 and __udivdi3 relocations unchanged; preserve recovered local assignment boundaries before blaming return-type extraction. (2026-09-27)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 baseline distinguishes operand order in nested min/max clamps: 0 > (127 < value ? 127 : value) ? 0 : (127 < value ? 127 : value) reproduces repeated upper-bound tests plus slt/beqz lower-bound test. Swapping comparisons to value < 127 and min < 0 changes branch sense and emits bgez. Reproduced MATCH 76/76 with correct line offsets in GAM_fn_vSetVolumeAmbiance, Voice and Music. (2026-09-27)

- [debug-format] MWCC debug local lists are in declaration order but the frame is allocated so later-declared locals get LOWER stack offsets; reversing a candidate declaration order to match the recorded stack offsets fixed MEC_fn_vGroundObstacleManagement vector placement (strict full-instruction similarity 0.43 -> 0.46). The recorded offsets read 0x10 above the actual slots. Locals that exist only inside macro expansions (block scope) are absent from the list but still occupy stack: a macro-private int at sp+0x418 alongside the function lObstacle at 0x410 moved every vector to its original slot (0.48 -> 0.60, frame and size then exact). (2026-09-27)

- [source-layout] For large functions, rebuild against the target line map instead of tuning: MEC_fn_vGroundObstacleManagement had several genuine logic errors from earlier attempts (wrong vector in a norm test, wrong argument to MEC_fn_bDetectStep and MEC_fn_vReportCollision, hCurrentMatrix vs hPreviousMatrix in collision calls, recomputed vs read-back translations). Walking the target line groups in order and fixing each against its instructions took opcode alignment 0.88 -> 0.91 and reached exact size and frame. A registerized vector (fields living in callee-saved FPRs across calls) is a local whose address is never taken; the unused float argument evidenced by a caller mov.s f12 is an omitted parameter. (2026-09-27)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 supports scalar __int128: four typed scalar loads and stores produce lq/sq without aggregate temporary spills. In VU1_RE_UpdateMatriceOutsideSDL, const volatile source accesses preserve the target four-load-before-four-store order; declaring v3,v2,v1,v0 colors t0/a3/a2/v1 exactly (36-byte MATCH). An aligned 64-byte struct copy instead emits a loop; four aligned 16-byte struct temporaries spill to stack. (2026-09-27)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 scoped optimization_level 0 reproduces the retail fn_vEncryptBuffer and fn_vDecryptBuffer loops exactly (128 bytes and line offsets): both parameters spill to 16-byte-spaced stack slots, ordinary int locals occupy saved s0/s1/s2, loop constants stay inside the loop, and delay slots remain nop. Same natural C at baseline level 3 is 72 bytes. No register keywords or assembly needed. (2026-09-27)

- [debug-format] The recovered tagGLcontext (PS2GL, copied into ~30 files) loses the 16-byte alignment of its float[4] vector members: the debug records put vScissorBox at +0xF0 (after a signed char at +0xE0), but plain float[4] compiles at +0xE4, so every later field sits 12 bytes too low. Retail impPS2glClearIndex/impPS2glReadBuffer write fIndexClearValue (+0x100) and eBufferReadMode (+0x12C) at their annotated offsets. Some recorded matches only work because workers picked whichever field name landed at the needed offset (impPS2glClearStencil writes bPointLighting, impPS2glLogicOp writes nTextureEnableBits). Fix: declare these vectors with a 16-byte-aligned vector type (extractor or per-file), then rename those fields. (2026-09-28)

- [compiler] DsgMem pointer getters return (unsigned char)(((unsigned int)p == 0u) ^ 1u): MWCC emits xor rd,rs,zero; sltiu 1; xori 1, matching the retail null test exactly. p != NULL, casts, ternaries and !(p == NULL) all produce different code. Matched fn_ucGetDsgVarComport/SOLinks/SuperObject/Light/SoundEvent. (2026-09-28)

- [debug-format] Extractor misses struct gaps whose size is not explained by natural alignment (e.g. tagGLcontext: signed char at +0xe0 then float[4] vScissorBox at +0xf0; unresolved aggregates emitted as 4-byte int placeholders). Compare each member's annotated +0x offset with its natural offset and insert explicit unsigned char padding; recompute any extractor tail padding (CAM_Shooter_ stride must be 0x2c0). Fixing tagGLcontext in 40 PS2GL TUs yielded 5 new matches and exposed 2 recorded matches that used wrong field names to compensate. (2026-09-28)

- [source-layout] Modules built without -g (no .debug/.line, e.g. GMT) can still be reconstructed: take names from .symtab, layouts from the same aggregates' debug records in other TUs, and GP globals from .symtab; the index accepts a hand-written /* addr: 0x... size: N bytes */ block. Boolean tests that end in sltiu/sltu/xori with no andi 0xff return int, not unsigned char, and callers returning the result must be int too (a uchar caller adds andi 0xff). For a mask test that emits xor+sltiu followed by an extra sltu, declaring initialised locals (x = field; t = x & mask; int b = (t == mask); return b != 0;) stopped MWCC folding the != 0 and matched the xor operand order. (2026-09-28)

- [relocation] Verifier fix: anonymous @N literals (strings, constants) were resolved by name against unrelated retail @N symbols, and HI16/LO16 inference required adjacent words; functions whose lui/addiu around a string were split by a scheduled instruction never matched. @N symbols are now never resolved by name; each HI16 pairs with the nearest following LO16, and unrelocated literals must match the retail bytes at the inferred address. The fix unlocked 12 previously-correct bodies. If a sprintf/printf-style function mismatches only on its format-string relocation, re-check before editing. (2026-09-28)

- [compiler] Baseline now includes -pragma "padloop on" (R5900 short-loop erratum workaround, listed next to fast_fptosi in mwccps2.exe's pragma table). Symptom: very short loops in the target contain extra nops before the back-branch (e.g. sb; lw; nop x4; bnez) that no source shape reproduces. Whole-repo test: +4 matches, 0 regressions. (2026-09-28)

- [compiler] Float zero tests: 'if (x)' emits c.eq.s $fX, $f0 (value first, zero second), while 'if (x != 0.0f)' and 'if (0.0f != x)' both emit c.eq.s $f0, $fX. When only the c.eq.s operand order differs, use the bare truth test. (2026-09-28)

- [compiler] Optimization-level probe on the largest function (MEC_fn_vGroundObstacleManagement, 8644 bytes): -opt level=4, level=4,intrinsics (-O4), speed,level=3, speed,level=4, space,level=3 and speed,level=4,intrinsics all produce code identical to level=3 (same 1910 differing words). The level choice is not the source of MEC's residual; level=3 stays the baseline. (2026-09-28)

- [compiler] Baseline now uses -strings reuse,nopool,readonly. Symptom: a short string literal (8 bytes or less) is addressed as addiu $aX, $gp, @N in the candidate but lui/addiu in the target. Retail never addresses literals through GP; readonly moves them out of .sdata. Whole-repo test: +8 matches (PLD_sz*Name family and others), 0 regressions. (2026-09-28)

- [source-layout] Some units auto-inlined: salloc.c's fn_vResetAllocator is inlined into fn_vInitAllocator yet emitted as a standalone symbol (called from transition.cxx). A non-static inline function emits no out-of-line copy under the baseline, so this needs #pragma auto_inline on in that file. Project-wide -inline auto breaks about 40 recorded matches, so treat it as per-file. Symptom: a target function contains the full body of a same-file sibling that also exists as its own symbol. Also: debug file attribution can be off by one function at object boundaries (fn_vResetAllocator was recorded under PS2Menu2D.c). (2026-09-28)

- [compiler] COP0 idioms: (1) COP0 Count reads (mfc0 $9) match with a statement-level asm { mfc0 var, $9 } (GLI_fn_vTimerPS2Counter; TimerPrv.c's fn_vTimerGetCounter is auto-inlined into its callers). (2) 'mtc0 $zero, $24' appears in about 40 game functions around draw/process calls (spimdisasm marks it handwritten). MWCC appends a hazard nop to an asm mtc0 unless the block begins with .set noreorder, so use asm { .set noreorder / mtc0 zero, $24 } and add an explicit nop only where the target has one (SHW_fn_vDrawShadow). (2026-09-28)

- [compiler] Baseline now includes -pragma "optimize_for_size off" (speed). Symptom: the target unrolls a loop the candidate keeps rolled (PS2M2D_vReinitMenuTextures clears 16 entries unrolled by 8). Adding it globally: +4 matches (also AGO2_ProcessSimpleParticleWithSpeedAndGravity, fn_v3dDataSetGlobalAccelerationFactor, GLI_vCleanAllSpecificData), 0 regressions in 1,894 recorded matches. The command-line spelling -opt speed,level=3 has no effect in this compiler, so earlier speed probes that used it were invalid; use the pragma. MEC_fn_vGroundObstacleManagement is unchanged under real speed. (2026-09-28)

- [debug-format] Extractor layout bug: it treats long/unsigned long as 4 bytes and inserts 4 bytes of padding after them, but on the EE long is 8 bytes. _stTimerCount (lo/hi unsigned long) grew from 16 to 24 bytes, shifting every tdstEngineStructure_ field after stEngineTimer by 0x10 in about 60 TUs. Removed 306 such paddings in 85 files (a padding field whose range starts 4 bytes into a long member). Earlier size workarounds that compensated (a_stGameAttributes3D[3] in AlwAct.c/GameDisp.c, a hard-coded +0xa8 in videodec.c) were restored. Diagnose with an offsetof probe copy of the TU: compile '(int)&((struct T*)0)->field' for every field and report the first that differs from its /* +0x */ annotation. (2026-09-28)

- [relocation] Matcher: function-local statics are emitted as name$N with per-compilation numbering (s_szFileName$11 vs retail $456). check now pairs them by base name plus size when exactly one retail symbol qualifies. Also: when a C++ TU calls printf and the relocation reads printf__FPCce, the declaration needs extern "C". (2026-09-28)

- [debug-format] Extractor emitted tduGetSetParam_ (the AI get/set value union) as a struct in 35 files (e.g. Function.c), giving each member its own offset (m_hWayPoint at +0x30, eType at +0x7c). Converted every struct whose two or more non-bitfield members are all at +0x0 into a union; packed-bitfield structs (all members 'bit N, byte +0x0') are genuine structs and were left alone. Symptom: accesses through p_stValue->uDummy.uParam.<member> land at large offsets. (2026-09-28)

- [debug-format] MTH4D_tdstVector_ is 16-byte aligned in retail (VU vectors), which makes MTH4D_tdstMatrix_ and MAT_tdstTransformation_ 16-aligned too. The extractor left the alignment out, so structs embedding a transformation after a 12-byte vector compiled 8 bytes short (e.g. DNM_stDynamicsBaseBlock.m_stPreviousMatrix at 0x78 instead of 0x80, pushing DNM_stDynamics blocks 0x14 early). Added __attribute__((aligned(16))) to all 183 MTH4D_tdstVector_ definitions; removed one misplaced compensating pad in MECOpt.c. Explicit _extractor_padding_*_align pads before aligned members are consistent and stay. (2026-09-28)

- [compiler] Level-0 comparison spelling: in unoptimized MWCC code, 'x > 7' emits 'slti $at, x, 8; bnez $at' (scratch register $at), while 'x >= 8' emits 'slti $v0, x, 8'. When only the slti destination ($at vs $v0) differs, rewrite the bound as '> N-1' or '< N'. mcmngr.c's range checks match with 'if ((x < 0) || (x > 7)) return 0;'. (2026-09-28)

- [compiler] mcmngr.c and vm_backup.c were built entirely at optimization level 0: a single file-level '#pragma optimization_level 0' matches every recorded function in both (keeping vm_backup.c's per-function 'peephole on' pragmas) and added 3 matches. Per-function level 0/1 pragmas had compensated for source differences: level-0 getters written with an extra 'iReturn' local look like level 1, and 'return x;' is correct. A per-file level-0 probe over all other files found no further candidates. (2026-09-28)

- [compiler] SVG level-0 files (mc_menu.c, vm_backup.c, mcmngr.c): calls through non-prototype declarations (e.g. 'extern int f();') reproduce retail argument evaluation order; a prototype that forces an argument conversion (int* -> unsigned int*, int -> char*) moves that argument's evaluation earlier. Under MWCC 3.0-011126, level 0 emits duplicate argument moves that retail never has; peephole on removes them but also strips lbu/lb extension ops retail keeps. MWCC 3.0b38-030307 matches VMS_fn_bFclose exactly and follows debug local order, but omits padloop/switch nops, so the retail build likely used an intermediate compiler build. (2026-09-29)

- [source-layout] PS2GL tagGLcontext: tag_GLlight (0x68 declared) and tag_GLmaterial need __attribute__((aligned(16))) (SDK sceVu0FVECTOR members); retail Lights[8] stride is 0x70. Without it every field after Lights (vcurNormal, pVBuf, ...) was 0x44-0x4C low in all 40 copies. (2026-09-29)

- [relocation] Verifier: R_MIPS_HI16 must include the paired LO16 immediate (nearest following LO16 on the same symbol); a sign-extended low half can carry into the high half (g_PS2glContext+0x60e0 -> lui 0x39, not 0x38). Fixed in match.py; 30 existing PS2GL/VU1 bodies matched with no source change. (2026-09-29)

- [source-layout] PS2GL_DataInput.c: MWCC emitted functions in reverse source order (debug line numbers decrease with address), so a callee inlined by a caller can sit after it in the extracted file; move it above and scope '#pragma auto_inline on' to its definition (file-wide auto_inline also inlines PS2glAssembleQuads, which retail calls). Array forms (glColor3fv etc.) evaluate components last-to-first: declare 'float r, g, b;' then assign 'b = v[2]; g = v[1]; r = v[0];' on the store line. (2026-09-29)

- [source-layout] C++ extractor output: derived classes carry only a '/* C++ base X at +0x0. */' comment, so their members compiled from offset 0 (e.g. AGO3_SimpleSparks::m_hArray at 0 instead of 0x30). Declare real inheritance ('struct D : X {'), with the base defined first; AGO3_Part also needs C_Vector aligned(16) so sizeof is 0x30. Converted in AGO3.cpp and 20 SND/SDL/FX files; 7 SND files still need manual fixes (compile errors). (2026-09-29)

- [compiler] DsgMem getters whose line map interleaves the eType store with the return test (store line, return line, store line, return line) match with 'if (h == NULL) return 0; return 1;' (or == -1 for hText) on one line, with both stores on one prior line; this gives xor/not + sltiu + xori in the jr delay slot. 'return h != -1' emits a single sltu and '!(h == -1)' / ternaries grow the function. Matched fn_ucGetDsgVarText/Action/Input. (2026-09-29)

- [compiler] A returned '? (char)1 : (char)0' ternary in an unsigned char function emits bne+nop, b with andi 0xff in the delay slot, then the false arm with its own andi and jr+nop (40 bytes); '? 1 : 0' emits the compact 28-byte bne/daddu/addiu/andi form. Matched GAM_fn_bIsEngineInLevelMode vs InTransitionMode (both read an int enum global). (2026-09-29)

- [compiler] GEO triangle face accessors (GEO_vSet/GetFaceOfIndexedTriangles, GEO_vSetIndexedUVOfFaceOfIndexedTriangles) add the a3_xIndex member offset to the list base before adding xIndexOfFace*6 (lw base; addiu +2; addu; sh 0). MWCC folds every ordinary form (p[i].a3_xIndex[1], pointer+const, char* casts) into the store offset; only re-typing the member address, ((GEO_tdstTripledIndex_*)(p->a3_xIndex + k))[i].a3_xIndex[0], keeps the offset unfolded and matches. The original was probably a macro that did the same. (2026-09-29)

- [compiler] MWCC materialises 'x <= K' as slti $at,x,K+1 with an unfilled nop delay slot, whereas 'x < K+1' uses a normal temp register and fills the slot. HIE_vSetCharactereRLI matched only with 'if (ucNbRLI <= 2 || ucNbRLI >= 0)'. (2026-09-29)

- [source-layout] clib.c PS2SQRTF is a hand-written asm function: retail is 'jr ra' (line 102) with 'sqrt.s f0,f12' in the delay slot (line 103), one line per instruction, and sqrt.s is not emittable from C (sqrtf/__builtin_sqrt becomes a fptodp+sqrt call). It matches as 'asm float PS2SQRTF(float x) { jr ra; sqrt.s f0, f12 }' on two lines, following the PS2GL_ApiTable asm-function precedent. (2026-09-29)

- [compiler] Retail boolean helpers probably return a ternary with typed TRUE/FALSE, e.g. 'cond ? (unsigned char)1 : (unsigned char)0' or '(char)1 : (char)0'. MWCC keeps the branchy form (v0=0 pre-set in the delay slot, v0=1, beq/bne to the exit, v0=0) and, with unsigned char arms in an unsigned char function, emits no trailing andi 0xff. Plain '? 1 : 0' collapses or adds andi. Matched IPT_fn_bIsJustValidated ((h && h->lState == 1) ? (unsigned char)1 : (unsigned char)0) and GAM_fn_bIsEngineInLevelMode ((char) arms). (2026-09-29)

- [compiler] Recurring retail shape: for p->arr[i] or p[i].field with a non-zero member offset, retail emits addiu base,+off and then adds the scaled index (the offset is not folded into the load/store). It shows up in GEO faces/cones, COL boxes, CollSet setters, PS2MC m_Flags and IPT device buttons. Casts such as ((T*)&p->arr)[i] or ((Elem*)&p->list->field)[i] reproduce the unfolded addiu, but the baseline often schedules the index scaling after the base add, and base/index registers can come out swapped. It is probably a compiler-build difference; only GEO faces and CollSet getters fully match so far. (2026-09-29)

- [source-layout] LST2 list-walk macros (get Nth element) match as a one-line 'for (i = 0, h = list.hFirstElementDyn; i < n; i++, h = h->hNextBrotherDyn) ;'. MWCC keeps the loop rolled, emits the guard as slt $at,zero,n and pads the short loop. Explicit while/do-while or if-guarded forms emit blez guards or get unrolled. Matched WPG_fn_hGetWaypointOfGraph. The same form leaves one padloop nop off in ObjType fn_ucFindPersonalIdOfPersonalType. (2026-09-29)

- [compiler] Loop-exit / branch-target nop family (fn_vReInitAllMiscFlagForAlwaysActiveState, SECT_fn_vInitActivationSector, CAM_fn_vResetArrayOfTransparentSuperObject, HIE_fn_hGetChildNumber): retail inserts a nop at a branch target (loop exit before jr/j, or the loop-condition label) that pushes the following instruction onto an 8-byte boundary. Our build never emits it. 19+ source shapes fail to produce it. mwccps2.exe lists an 'alignlabel' pragma next to padloop, but '#pragma alignlabel on/8/16' changes nothing in this build (padloop off does change code), so it is probably behaviour of the retail compiler build. (2026-09-30)

- [source-layout] C++ virtual methods: the extractor drops 'virtual' and emits each class vtable as an 'unsigned int __vt__N<class>;' global. Retail virtual calls load the vptr (after the base data members, e.g. this+0x10 for C_SdlRef) and jump through a slot (first virtual at +8). Declare the methods virtual in the base class, in retail slot order, and delete the __vt__ placeholders (MWCC then emits the real vtables). In SDLRefs.cpp this matched 13 ProcessNoInterpol/ProcessWithInterpol wrappers written as 'Process();'. Also: extracted '__dl(void* ptr)' members are 'operator delete' (implicitly static), not instance methods. (2026-09-30)

- [compiler] Indexed member access with a swapped base/index register pair (retail: base pointer in v0, scaled index in v1, addu v0,v0,v1) matched by taking the element address into a pointer local first: 'p = &obj->a_xArray[i]; x = *p;' (or '*p = v;'). Found by the permuter for fn_xStandardGameGetCustomFloatSO; the same edit matched the Get/SetCustomLongSO and SetCustomFloatSO siblings. Direct a[i], pointer arithmetic, i[a] and casts all keep the swapped allocation. (2026-09-30)

- [compiler] Retail aligns branch-target labels inside and just after loops to 8 bytes with a nop: across the retail ELF, nops at branch targets sit at addr%8==4 in 3487/3535 cases. The padding rate is 96% for loop-exit labels, 77% for labels inside a loop body, 29% for loop heads and 0% (24/9426) for targets outside any loop. 962 of 5501 functions contain such a nop. Our MWCC 3.0-011126 build never emits it: '#pragma alignlabel on/8' compiles but changes nothing, even on in-loop branch probes. This explains the loop-exit/branch-target nop family (AlwAct, SECT, CAM, HIE, GLI_vRemoveCharacterVAFromProcessList) and is a compiler-build difference, not a source shape. (2026-10-01)

- [source-layout] SND_R3 sources: the extractor drops C++ namespace usnd, so functions taking usnd classes (CResData, CVar, CUUID, ...) mangle without Q24usnd and report OBJECT_ERROR. Fix per file by replacing 'typedef struct X X;' with 'namespace usnd { struct X; } typedef usnd::X X;' and defining 'struct usnd::X {...}' (164 usnd class names come from Q2?usnd in config/symbols.txt; leave template types such as stree/smap/TTypedVar alone). Matched SwitchData::f_bFindData's symbol and CMusicMarkerNotifTarget::fv_vVarDestroyed. Nested classes such as CExtraEventActor::CResObjParams (Q216...) must also be real nested classes, with base usnd::CInterface (virtuals fv_vRelease at +8, fv_vAddRef at +0xc). (2026-10-01)

- [compiler] VU0 lqc2/sqc2 4x4 matrix copies (GLI_vSaveCameraMatrix, CHEAP_StoreMatrix: one line per copy, lqc2 vf1-vf4 then sqc2 vf1-vf4) are not emittable from C; an inline C++ wrapper inline void PS2_M_vCopyMatrix(float (*_src)[4], float (*_dst)[4]) { asm { lqc2 vf1,0x0(_src) ... sqc2 vf4,0x30(_dst) } } matches with -inline on. Parameter order matters: src first gives the retail v1=src, a0=dst allocation; dst-first is only EQUIVALENT. (2026-10-01)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0 has '#pragma dividefix' (listed next to padloop in mwccps2.exe's pragma table). Symptom: retail inserts two nops between a div.s and a following jal/branch (retail survey: 2 nops before jal 105x vs 0 nops 2x; before branches 98x vs 76x), which no source shape reproduces. Scoped '#pragma dividefix on'/'reset' around GLI_vCleanInterpolData (Init_PS2GL.c) gives MATCH 184/184. Not yet tested as a whole-repo baseline flag. (2026-10-03)

- [compiler] Baseline now applies '-pragma "dividefix on"' to game sources only (src/Rayman3/Disk_01/R3PS2_SCEA_engine_sources; tools/match.py mwcc_flags and the Makefile GAME_MWCC_FLAGS). Evidence: retail game code has two nops between div.s and a following branch/call at 202 of 204 sites, PS2GL library code at 3 of 117. Whole-repo test: +2 game matches (GLI_fn_vSetBrightness, GLI_vDoCompleteFade), 0 game regressions; enabling it globally loses 89 PS2GL_DataInput/ListsRecPly matches. Exceptions in retail: AGO.c CreateType104AGO and camera.cxx GLI_xAdjustCameraToViewport2. verify-recorded 2265/2265. (2026-10-03)

- [relocation] Local-static relocations are now paired by the address in the retail instruction (GPREL16, or the HI16/LO16 pair, minus the candidate's addend) with a matching base name, before the old unique-name+size rule. This resolves ambiguous names such as C++ guards (init$85 -> init$1242), duplicated names (SAV2_NotInitialized$1330/$1342) and the retail 'name$localstaticN$Function' spelling used by some C units (Toto$localstatic3$CheckData). Unlocked SND_fn_hGetCurrentSoundHandle and fn_p_stSAV2_DoVarsNeedToBeSet. (2026-10-03)

- [compiler] Engine float-to-int macro: retail converts with 'cvt.w.s $f30, x; mfc1 r, $f30' at 164 sites in 77 game functions (f30 is callee-saved, so the function also saves/restores it; PS2GL never does this). MWCC never chooses f30 itself. Reproduce with a file-level 'inline long MTH_M_xRealToLong(float _xValue) { long _lResult; asm { cvt.w.s f30, _xValue / mfc1 _lResult, f30 } return _lResult; }' (Procedur.c, Getset.c). A dead result still keeps the asm: fn_p_stSFX_ActivateLightReflection matches with 'long lValue; if (stParam.eType == E_vt_Float) lValue = MTH_M_xRealToLong(stParam.uDummy.uParam.xValue);', using a real tdstGetSetParam_ local instead of the extractor's int stParam[4]. (2026-10-03)

- [compiler] Conditional-branch delay-slot fill difference (recurring residual): our MWCC copies the first instruction of a function's return block (e.g. 'move v0,s0', 'move v0,zero', 'li v0,1' before the epilogue) into a beqz/bne delay slot, where retail leaves nop and keeps that instruction at the branch target. Seen in fni_CompareIntegerLess/Greater, fn_hReturnHandleMicroFromIndex, fn_p_stSetVSyncWait, fn_p_stTraceProcedure. Retail does fill conditional slots from targets elsewhere (1117 game sites vs 10503 nop), so this is a narrower heuristic difference, not a missing mode; none of the scoped pragmas (schedule, peephole, conditional_move, tailcall, etc.) changes it. (2026-10-03)

- [compiler] Small MWCC switch compare chains: the order of the beq tests depends on the source case order (PCS_fn_hGetGeoObjOfPhysicalCollSet: cases written 0,1,3,2 give retail's tests 2,3,1,0), so permute case order before anything else. A recurring unfixed residual in 4-way chains is that retail leaves the second beq's delay slot as nop where ours fills it with the next compare's li (VIDSPE_fn_vSetVideoQuality, fn_vSetVectorComponentForAffectOperator, PCS_fn_hGetGeoObjOfPhysicalCollSet); no case order, default or fallthrough variant changes it. (2026-10-03)

- [source-layout] MWCC fills an early-return branch's delay slot only from code that precedes the test in source order. When retail has a fall-through-path load in the delay slot of the first conditional branch (e.g. 'bnez v1,ret; lwc1 f3,gravity'), the source reads that field into a local BEFORE the early-return test. Evidence: MEC_fn_vGravitySpeed matched with 'xGravity = p->m_xGravity;' placed before 'if (flag) return;'. Writing the read after the test leaves a nop in the slot, and the register choices change with it. (2026-10-03)

- [compiler] Retail inlines float-to-UNSIGNED conversion; our MWCC 3.0-011126 calls fptoui even under fast_fptosi. The retail sequence is 'lui 0x4f00; mtc1; c.le.s 2^31,x; bc1t big; cvt.w.s; mfc1; b done; big: sub.s x,x,2^31; cvt.w.s; mfc1; or r,r,0x80000000' (e.g. SVG_fn_s32Format: fn_vTimerWait((unsigned)(fn_ulTimerWaitTicksPerSecond()/1000.0f))). The retail ELF has no fptoui call sites at all, so any (unsigned)float cast is a compiler-build residual, not a source-shape problem. (2026-10-03)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 'long' is 64-bit, so a (long) cast on an unsigned char value stored to a 32-bit int field emits a re-narrowing 'andi r,r,0xff' even when the value is already zero-extended (from lbu or a uchar call result). The AI getter/setter store 'p_stValue->uDummy.uParam.lValue = (long)bValue; p_stValue->eType = E_vt_Integer;' needs that cast to match (CAM_fn_p_stGetSetMirrorFlag). A 'long' local assigned a uchar and stored to an int field instead gives dsll32/dsra32. (2026-10-03)

- [compiler] Game sources now build with MWCC 3.0.3-020716 (decomp.me compilers repo); PS2GL stays on 3.0-011126. Full sweep of all recorded matches and attempts: game +45/-21 (the 3.0.3 build fixes the eager conditional delay-slot fill, the return-block move fill, inline float-to-unsigned conversion and some padloop over-padding), PS2GL -90/+10 (int-to-float conversions and padding), so the compiler is chosen per owner. Retail game code is not exactly either build: it pads short loops to 7 instructions before the back-branch, where 3.0 over-pads loops containing an inner branch and 3.0.3 under-pads straight loops (11 demoted functions). The other 3.x builds (3.0.1-020123, 3.0b22/b38/b50/b52, 3.0.1b44) were probed: b-series lose matches; 3.0.1 is sweep-pending. (2026-10-03)

- [compiler] Correction to the 3.0.3 entry: the game baseline is MWCC 3.0.1-020123, not 3.0.3. Game-only sweep: 3.0.1 matches 1,626 with zero losses against the old 3.0-011126 set; 3.0.3 matches 1,619 but loses 21 (padloop under-padding etc.); 21 functions match only under 3.0.1 and 16 only under 3.0.3, so retail sits between the two. Every PS2 CodeWarrior release on archive.org (cw_consoles: R2.5, R3.01, R3.03, R3.03J, R3.04, R3.5, R3.6 hotfixes) was extracted: R3.03/R3.03J = 3.0.3-020716, R3.04 = 3.0b22-020926, R3.5 = 3.0b38 + 3.0.1b44, R3.6 hotfixes = 3.0.1b75+. Nothing new exists between 020123 and 020716. (2026-10-03)

- [compiler] Per-file compiler: game files listed in config/compiler-3.0.3-sources.txt build with MWCC 3.0.3-020716, the rest with 3.0.1-020123 (match.py owner_compiler and the Makefile both read the list). Across 28 files with a compiler-dependent difference, no file had matches unique to both builds, which fits per-file builds made at different times. When a function's only residual is scheduling, try 'MWCC=Compiler/mwcps2-3.0.3-020716/mwccps2.exe python3 tools/match.py check F'. If it matches, add the file only when every recorded match in it also matches under 3.0.3 (then run verify-recorded). First batch: 14 files, +17 matches, 0 lost. (2026-10-03)

- [compiler] (Superseded 2026-10-04 by '-inline deferred' for game sources.) Under '-inline on', auto_inline is decided at the callee definition and dont_inline around a caller keeps that caller out of line. Under deferred inlining caller-side pragmas no longer work; the MSWay.c pattern (MSWay_fn_vDesinitPath expanded in fn_vMSWayDesinit/fn_vMSWayFree/the first call in fn_vMSWayCopyClone, called in fn_vMSWayInit/fn_vMSWayAlloc/the second CopyClone call) is reproduced by a one-line statement macro MSWay_M_vDesinitPath with no pragmas, matching all six. (2026-10-04)

- [compiler] Retail game objects list functions in reverse source-line order (e.g. CollSet.c, MSWay.c: ascending addresses carry descending debug line ranges), which is what MWCC '-inline deferred' produces (probe: a,b,c are emitted c,b,a). Our sources keep ELF order under '-inline on', so a retail callee that was EARLIER in the original source appears LATER in our file and cannot be inlined (CS_fn_eGetPrivilegedActivationZone inlines CS_fn_ulGetPrivilegedActivationZoneInCollSet). Whole-game sweep: '-inline deferred' gains/loses nothing except scoped dont_inline workarounds; 'auto,deferred' loses 124 matches and gains 0. (2026-10-04)

- [compiler] MWCC small switch (compare chain, no jump table): the beq tests come out in REVERSE order of the case labels as written, with case 0 tested last by beqz; labels that share a body but appear in separate groups (e.g. 'case 0: case 2: case 3: break; case 1: ...; break; case 4: break;') change the order. Read the source label order straight off the target's compare sequence (fn_vMSSoundInit, fn_vSectInfoDesinit, fn_vStdGameDesinit). Listing a case 0 that only breaks can turn a 5-label chain into a jump table. (2026-10-04)

- [compiler] Missing game flag found: '-O3,p' (UNIX-style optimisation spelling: level 3 + speed) is NOT equivalent to '-opt level=3' plus '#pragma optimize_for_size off'. With it MWCC pads loop-head branch targets at 4 mod 8 with a nop (the former 'alignment-nop residual', ~880 unmatched game functions) and changes some scheduling (fn_vReputGameViewportCamera, IPT_fn_swConvertAZERTYtoQWERTY). Whole-game sweep: +48, none lost; the PS2GL library loses 89. After adopting it the 3.0.3 per-file list shrank from 14 to 10 files. Lead came from Dark Chronicle's mwcps2 '-O4,p'. Asm-nop workarounds are no longer needed. (2026-10-04)

- [source-layout] AI procedure handlers (switch on (p_stTree-1)->uDummy.uId.eProcedureId): when retail returns the fn_p_stEvalTree result straight from v0 (no move to a saved register, each case ending 'b epilogue; nop' or falling into it), the source has 'return p_stTree;' inside each case rather than 'break;'. When retail moves it to s0/s1 and ends with 'move v0,sN' after the switch, use break. Matched 6 Procedur.c handlers (Brain/Portal activation, DataPath, DisplaySegment, MenuAndVignette). Single-label 'if (eProcedureId == X)' bodies are usually a one-case switch (beq to the case, b to the return). (2026-10-04) (2026-10-04)

- [source-layout] Unfolded member offset in indexed struct-array access (retail 'addiu base,base,OFF; addu base,base,idx*size; lw/sw 0(base)' instead of 'lw/sw OFF(base)'): MWCC folds the offset for list[i].f, (list+i)->f, *(list+i) and every local-pointer form, and still folds it with '#pragma peephole off' (which also unfolds unrelated accesses), so it is not a flag. Only taking the field address before indexing reproduces the instruction set: ((T*)((char*)list + OFF))[i*n], ((T*)&list->f)[i*n], (S*)&list->f + i, or I*size + (int)&list->f; 18 recorded matches use these spellings (GEO_vSetPointsOfIndexedCone, COL_vGetMinMaxPointOfIndexedAlignedBox, GEO_vSetFaceOfIndexedTriangles). Single-access functions written this way (GEO_vSet*OfIndexed*, COL_hGetGameMaterialOfIndexed*, CollSet setters, PS2MC flags, ISI getters) stay register/scheduling-different from retail, and source operand order, index/element locals, -O2/O4/s, other compiler builds and a permuter run did not fix that. (2026-10-05) (2026-10-04)

- [source-layout] AGO3.cpp: AGO3_Part is polymorphic. Retail calls parts through a vtable pointer at +0x28 with DesInit at slot +0x0c, Process at +0x10 and Display at +0x14 (AGO3_vKill, AGO3_vProcessAll, AGO3_vDisplayAll), so the base declares 'virtual ~AGO3_Part(); virtual void DesInit(); virtual void Process(); virtual void Display(GLD_tdstViewportAttributes_*)'. Derived fields still start at +0x30, and every recorded AGO3 match verified unchanged. External engine calls from this C++ file need extern "C" prototypes, or the relocation names come out mangled (e.g. AGO2_MEM_GetPointerFromHandle__FUi). (2026-10-05) (2026-10-04)

- [source-layout] MEC obstacle filters (MECTools.c) inline small boolean helpers that keep their own temporaries: a material-type test that loads hCollideMaterial into its own register, and a null-vector test (|x|<0.001f && |y|<0.001f && |z|<0.001f using an abs.s inline). Writing them as file-level inline functions reproduced MEC_fn_vDeleteNoCollidedGroundFace and MEC_fn_vDeleteNullTranslationObstacle; the same condition written in place allocates one fewer register. Translation copies from a stack vector into a matrix (MAT set-translation) need the union word-copy idiom. (2026-10-05) (2026-10-04)

- [debug-format] Retail vtables are readable from the ELF: __vt__<len><Class> symbols (e.g. __vt__9AGO3_Part at its symbol address) hold two zero words and then the virtual function addresses in slot order, which resolve to symbol names via the ELF symbol table. That recovers virtual method order and names where the extractor emitted plain structs: AGO3_Part is DesInit, Kill_Enabled, Process, Display. Use it before guessing vtable slots from 'lw t9,0x28(obj); lw t9,N(t9); jalr' call sites. (2026-10-05) (2026-10-04)

- [source-layout] C++ 'new Class(args)' factories (SDLAccess.cpp SDL_REF_GetNew*Ref) match only when the classes have their real virtuals and inline constructors: the base constructor stores its vtable and fields, then the derived one overwrites the vtable and sets its own fields, all inlined after the class operator new call (__nw__8C_SdlRefFUi) and its null check. Field store order follows constructor statement order (C_SdlRef stores m_uIndexInList before m_uRefType). Object size comes from the 'li a0,size' before operator new: a float[4][4] member needed __attribute__((aligned(16))) to reach the retail 0x70. 'delete p' with an inline destructor (vtable store, then Destruct()) reproduces SDL_Destroy's double null check. (2026-10-05) (2026-10-04)

- [source-layout] Extractor padding often marks a lost vtable pointer. SND_R3: CResObjParams derives from the polymorphic usnd::CInterface, so its vtable pointer is at +0x0 and fields start at +0x4; the extracted structs started at +0x0, making CExtraEventActor (and every struct embedding it) 4 bytes short wherever no '+0x64' padding compensated. CLinkActor's '_extractor_padding_00 +0x8' is its vtable pointer: declaring 'virtual ~CLinkActor();' at that position puts it there, because MWCC places the vtable pointer where the first virtual is declared. A trailing virtual put AGO3_Part's at its end (+0x28). Also check the mangled parameter types: retail CLinkActor methods take 'const usnd::CEventResData*' (PCQ24usnd13CEventResData), and a plain CEventResData* gives OBJECT_ERROR. (2026-10-05) (2026-10-04)

- [source-layout] Look for inlined callers before writing duplicate code. CLinkActor::f_vInit is mfn_poAddExtraEventActorInstance(-1, NULL) inlined; both functions reference the same guarded local static (s32_InstanceId$970 / init$971), and the Add path inlines mfn_poGetExtraEventActorInstance. Writing Add with its static, then 'auto_inline on' around Add and Get, matched all three, including f_vInit's line map. When two functions touch the same function-local static, one is an inline expansion of the other. The verifier pairs local statics by address, so compiler numbering (init$70 vs init$971) does not matter. (2026-10-05) (2026-10-04)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0.1 (C++): an unsigned-char flag tested as a loop condition with 'bRet == 0' re-extends it before every test (andi v1,v0,0xff; beqz v1), and '!bRet' tests the raw register. CLinkTypeActor::mfn_bSoundPlaying matched only with == 0 (or !(bRet & 0xff)). Extracted SND_R3 STL wrappers declare sallocator as int data[4]; the real allocator is empty (1 byte at stree+0xc), so each smap/sset is 0x10 too large. An empty sallocator plus CActorModifiers/CResObj/CActorProgramContext vptrs put CActorResObj::m_oProgramContext at the retail +0xac. (2026-10-04)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0.1 (C++): when retail loads an array element once into a saved register before a search loop and reloads it for a later use (SwitchMgr::fs_vUpdateWaves: s3 = ms_pDel[i] for f_bFindData, then ms_pDel[i] again for delete), the search was an inlined helper taking the element as a parameter. Copying the element into a plain local first only reaches EQUIVALENT (different register numbering); a static inline finder function MATCHes. (2026-10-04)

- [abi] SND_R3 usnd STL: map iteration matches retail mangled names with a small template set in namespace usnd. It has: streebase, with nested sTreeLink and iterator_base, whose (sTreeLink*) ctor and _Inc() are out of line; stree<K,V,KFN,CMP,ALLOC> : streebase, with an inline iterator (operator* through sTreeNode::m_oValue at +0x10, operator!= as !(a==b)), out-of-line sTreeLink*& _Lmost() and inline begin()/end(); and smap<K,T,CMP,ALLOC> with a nested empty _Kfn and stree<K, spair<const K,T>, _Kfn, CMP, ALLOC> _Tr. CLinkTypeActorCont derives from usnd::smap<int, CLinkTypeActor*, usnd::less<int>, usnd::sallocator<CLinkTypeActor*> >. Extracted files keep flat global smap/stree stand-ins: qualify smap members inside usnd:: structs as ::smap so the template name does not hide them. Reproduced SwitchMgr::fs_vStopAll exactly. (2026-10-04)

- [compiler] SND_R3 usnd containers (renamed Dinkumware xtree): define the template members in the class body and let MWCC's inliner choose. Its depth/budget limits reproduce retail's mixed outcomes from natural code, e.g. CSoundSemaphoreCont::mfn_roGetInstance (static local singleton) leaves _Init out of line, and ~CSoundSemaphoreCont calls _Lmost and _GetHead out of line. The stree iterator needs a user copy constructor written as iterator(const iterator& o) : iterator_base(o._Ptr) {}: retail passes iterators to erase(iterator, iterator) by address and copies through the out-of-line iterator_base(sTreeLink*) ctor. Default arguments ALLOC() and CMP() show up as a 1-byte zero bss @N copy (value-initialized POD sallocator) and an uninitialized temp (less<T> derives from binary_function). #pragma inline_depth(3) also reproduced CSoundSemaphore's ctor, but as a global flag it broke 2 recorded SND matches, so it is not the retail setting. (2026-10-04)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0.1 -O3,p contracts plain C a*b - c*d float expressions into R5900 mula.s + msub.s, scheduling them freely, e.g. into a jalr delay slot. CLinkMicro::mfn_bApplyPositionAndVelocity's cross-product arguments reproduce retail's mula/msub instructions without asm. An asm-block helper instead acts as a scheduling barrier and mismatches, so check plain C before using the accumulator-asm exception for msub. (2026-10-05)

- [compiler] Unfolded-offset family, extra data point (SND sndeethrd usnd_vEnter/LeaveCritSect): it appears on the first array access whose address is CSE'd into a register reused for both load and store (gs_uiCSCount[uiCS-1]--, then a re-test). Retail emits li at,-4; addu base,base,at; addiu base,base,0; addu p,base,idx*4. Later uncached accesses in the same function fold normally (gs_uiCSThread+0x3fc). Making the arrays static or respelling the index ((a-1)[i], *(&a[i]-1), a[i+-1]) does not reproduce it. (2026-10-05)

- [compiler] Jump-to-test loop entry (b to the test, bottom bnez) is reproduced for busy-wait loops whose body is only an inline asm COP0 write, written as while (flag) SND_VID_vClearCop0Register24(); with asm { mtc0 zero, $24 }. The polled flags must be volatile, or MWCC hoists the load out of the loop. Matched SND_VID_fn_vResumeVideoSound; the retail ELF has 142 such mtc0 zero,$24 writes, including the unresolved FireFx/GrassFx/AGO jump-to-test loops, so they may share this cause. (2026-10-05)

- [source-layout] Loop bounds written as sizeof(a)/sizeof(a[0]) with an int counter give an unsigned compare (sltiu) and, after MWCC unrolls by 8, a remainder loop entered by jumping to its test. A literal bound (int or unsigned counter) instead emits a sltiu/beqz guard before the remainder. This matched FreeMenu2DTextures exactly, so for unrolled jump-to-test loops over arrays, try a sizeof bound first. (2026-10-05)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 3.0.1 lowers a small sparse switch (no jump table) as beq tests in reverse source order of the case labels: case 2 / case 1 / case 4 in source emits tests for 4, 1, 2 (fn_v3dDataDesinit). Use the retail test order reversed to order the cases, and keep empty 'case X: break;' labels, which still get their own test. (2026-10-05)

- [source-layout] Extractor fields typed int that are really pointers (e.g. GEO_tdstGeometricObject_::d_xListOfElementsTypes) change codegen when indexed through a cast: ((short*)obj->field)[i] emits addu idx,base (operand order swapped), while a real pointer field, or *(short**)&obj->field, emits addu base,idx as in retail. Retype such fields to their pointer type when an index addu is the only residual. (2026-10-05)

- [source-layout] Jump-table switches with nothing after them: when retail leaves the range-check beqz delay slot as nop (instead of hoisting the table lui into it), each case ends in 'return;' rather than 'break;' (impPS2glGetPointerv matched byte-for-byte with lines ok; default:/int-cast variants did not change it). (2026-10-05)

- [source-layout] Long if/return threshold chains that retail compiles with slti into $at (not v0) and the return value loaded in the branch delay slot are one multi-line ternary 'return a ? 13 : b ? 12 : ... : ln > 2;', one line per arm; the if/return form uses v0 for the compare and leaves nops (GLI_lGetLog2n MATCH). (2026-10-05)

- [compiler] Vector norm idiom (retail mula.s/madda.s/madd.s then sqrt.s): load the three components into scalar float locals, then use file-local MTH_SQR3_TO(result,x,y,z) and MTH_SQRT_TO(r,v) asm macros (copied from DNMCame.c). Plain C x*x+y*y+z*z gives mul/adda/madd instead. MWCC itself adds the sqrt.s hazard nops, so the macros contain no nop. GEO_fn_vComputeBoundingSphereOfOneSphereAndOnePoint matched first try with lines ok; COL_fn_vEnlargeSphereIfNecessary reaches 208/208 with an FP scheduling residual. (2026-10-05)

- [source-layout] Switch whose no-match path retail ends with 'b join; nop' (not stealing the join's first instruction into the delay slot): add an explicit 'default: x = <value it already holds>; break;'. In SAI_fn_p_vGetTableValuePointerOf, 'default: p_vReturn = NULL; break;' (redundant after p_vReturn = NULL) gives the exact nop; an empty default, 'default: break;', or a return does not. (2026-10-05)

- [compiler] Compare spelling picks the slti destination register: 'x <= 1' compiles to 'slti $at,x,2' while 'x < 2' gives 'slti v1,x,2' (same immediate, different register). Retail $at in a slti/branch pair means the source wrote the inclusive form '<= N-1' (or '> N-1', as in GLI_lGetLog2n's ternary). GLI_vRealyComputeLod matched with '<= 1'. (2026-10-05)

- [compiler] Unresolved residual family, stack-address CSE: retail reaches an address-taken stack local (vector copied with MTH3D int-word copy macros, *(int*)&v.xY) with sp-relative offsets, while MWCC 3.0.1 here materializes &v.xY/&v.xZ (or &v) into registers when they are used more than once. Field, array and struct-copy spellings all keep some materialized address; MWCC 3.0.3 removes only the &v.xX one. Seen in MEC_fn_vReportCollideActor and WP_fnv_WayPoint_ComputeLocation (MatSp.h inline). (2026-10-05)

- [debug-format] Extracted local declaration order is often reversed relative to the original. When a candidate is EQUIVALENT with two same-typed locals' registers swapped, reverse their declaration order first. Matched after swapping: fn_vLastDesinitFamilyStructure (p_stNextFamily/param0), fn_vDesinitFamilyStructureForPriority, INT_fn_bFastRejectIntersectSemiaxeWithSphere (xMax/xMin), GLI_xCreateMaterial (loop pointers). (2026-10-05)

- [compiler] Stack/global address CSE residual (MEC_fn_vReportCollideActor, WP, GEO culling family) is partly a compiler-build difference: MWCC 3.0.3 folds *(int*)&global.field and *(int*)&local.field into direct %lo / sp-offset addressing where 3.0.1 materializes the address. GEO/Culling.c (with ((int*)&v)[i] source reads for the base-pointer form) and AI/AIBase/WPWayPt.c each gain one match under 3.0.3 with nothing lost, so both were added to config/compiler-3.0.3-sources.txt. MECCol.c still differs under 3.0.3. (2026-10-10)

- [compiler] The recurring 'float-to-unsigned conversion inlined in retail but a jal fptoui here' residual is a compiler-build difference: MWCC 3.0.3-020716 inlines (unsigned int)float as c.le.s 2^31 / trunc.w.s / or 0x80000000 exactly like retail, while 3.0.1 and 3.0 call fptoui (fn_uiGetSomeBytes goes from 396/428 to 424/428). When an fptoui residual remains, test the owning file under 3.0.3 (MWCC=... check) and apply the per-file 3.0.3 criterion. (2026-10-10)

- [compiler] Compiler/flag sweeps (2026-10-10): MWCC 3.0-011126 with the game flags gains nothing in any game file with attempts, so that lead is closed. PS2GL with -O3,p is per-file: PS2GL_Pipeline.c (+2) and PS2GL_VU1_Pipeline.c (+1) gain with no losses and now take it via config/library-O3p-sources.txt, while DataInput.c (-76) and ListsRecPly.c (-13) must not. The alignment-nop residual in other library files is worth checking against that list. (2026-10-10)

- [compiler] Unfolded element-offset family (retail computes &a[i].field as (a+fieldoff)+i*size: fn_bGetVectorFromPhonem, J_New_GetInterpolPointOnEdge, INT_fn_vViewAndAddFaceElementInList, GLI_vSetParaSphericalLight): under 3.0.1 the spelling ((T*)&a[0].field)[i*stride] reproduces the unfolded addressing, but MWCC 3.0.3 folds it back into the load/store offset. Pragmas opt_propagation/strength_reduction/lifetimes/dead_assignments/loop_invariants change nothing, and opt_common_subs off makes it worse. Functions needing both this and 3.0.3-style stack addressing point to an intermediate compiler build we don't have. (2026-10-10)

- [compiler] MWCC 3.0.3 schedules ordinary instructions (e.g. a lui/mtc1 for a 0.5f constant) across an inline-function asm { sqrt.s } where 3.0.1 treats the asm as a barrier. MAT_fn_v_MatrixToQuat (inline MTH_xSqrt wrapper, locals in reversed debug order) matches only under 3.0.3, so MatPS2.c moved to the 3.0.3 list. When a sqrt.s/asm function differs only by constants loaded after the asm, test 3.0.3. (2026-10-10)

- [source-layout] The preflight cache (list --compileable) was stale: 'python3 tools/match.py preflight --jobs 4' now reports 356/356 sources compileable and the unmatched-compileable pool grew from 42 to 2523 functions (729 small unattempted). Re-run preflight after source-wide header and struct work. PS2GL display-list recorders (recPS2gl*) follow one template: store into g_PS2glContext.valist*[0], then 'if (!g_PS2glContext.bFinalCompile) PS2glSaveListCall(code, &field)'. The *fv forms read components into temporaries in reverse order (r = v[2]; t = v[1]; s = v[0];). (2026-10-10)

- [relocation] HI16/LO16 pairing: MWCC lists each HI16 right before the LO16 that consumes it in the relocation table, even when another LO16 of the same symbol sits between them in the code (recPS2glMultiTexCoord4fvARB: lui $at for a later lbu %lo(g_PS2glContext+0x6118), with an addiu %lo(g_PS2glContext) in between). The linker pairs by table order (retail %hi = 0x39). match.py paired with the nearest following LO16 by offset (0x38) and rejected byte-identical code. The candidate resolver now pairs by relocation-table order, falling back to offset order; all recorded matches still verify and the two PS2GL multi-texture 4fv recorders now match. (2026-10-10)

- [compiler] MWCC PS2 (3.0, 3.0.1, 3.0.3, 3.0b38) schedules individual instructions out of a statement-level asm {} block: in PS2_dl_cosf/sinf the first abs.s/sub.s (depending only on $f12 or one constant) is hoisted above prologue swc1 saves and constant mtc1s, while retail keeps the whole block after them. .set noreorder, a leading label, one asm statement per instruction, extra scopes, #pragma scheduling off and -O2/O3/O4 do not stop it. Also: a C local named f4/f24 etc. inside asm {} refers to the hardware register, not the variable, so the C assignments to it are dead-stripped. (2026-10-10)

- [compiler] Deferred inlining (-inline deferred with #pragma auto_inline on) inlines a later-defined small function into an earlier caller only if the callee is defined after the pragma. TimerPrv.c: fn_ulTimerEventTickPerSecond (line 89) stays a call in TMR_fn_wInitLibrary (line 17) when it is defined before '#pragma auto_inline on', while fn_vTimerGetCounter (line 113) is still inlined into its callers. Moving the pragma alone (callee still after it) does not help; the callee definition position relative to the pragma decides. (2026-10-10)

- [source-layout] Every .cxx unit's debug records say Language: C, and retail C function-local statics with constant initializers have no C++ init guard (lb init$N / sb). The build compiles .cxx as C++, which adds the guard. '#pragma cplusplus off' at the top of the unit (with extern "C" removed) fixes it: transition.cxx fn_vOneTrameThread's iOnce$404 matched and the other 11 functions were unchanged. Symptom: a candidate guard sequence that retail lacks, in a .cxx file. Real .cpp units (SND_R3) do have guards. (2026-10-10)

- [source-layout] MWCC evaluates plain call arguments after argument calls: f(p->a->b, g(p)) loads p->a->b after g returns. Retail code that loads the field chain first and holds it in an s-register across g came from an inline accessor; inline calls are evaluated left-to-right with real calls. Matched fn_vBrainInit (Brain.c) with a static inline mind getter for fn_vSecondPassOfMindLoading(mind, fn_bIsInFix(p), e). (2026-10-10)

- [compiler] MWCC dividefix pads a div.s with nops before a join label only when the join is a real if/else merge. 'x = 0; y = d; if (c) { ...; x = a / b; y = e / b; }' drops retail's 2+3 nops after the two div.s. Moving the defaults into 'else { x = 0; y = d; }' restores them, and the compiler coalesces the else away with no extra code. Separately, 'if (f)' on a float gives c.eq.s f,zero where 'f != 0.0f' gives c.eq.s zero,f. Matched GLI_vSetFog2. (2026-10-10)

- [source-layout] A whole if/else block of calls mapped to one line-map line is an inlined static helper, not inline code. read.c videoCallback's memcpy chain (offsets 0xac-0x16c, all line 58) is ezmpeg's static inline copy2(pd0,d0,pd1,d1,ps0,s0,ps1,s1), and the early 'return 0' becomes the len=0 path. Sony SDK sample sources (ezmpeg read.c/strfile.c, libgifpk/libdma helpers) give names and statement order; debug local lists come out in reverse declaration order, nested-scope locals included. After shape matched, a script over local declaration permutations (60 tries) resolved the remaining register allocation. (2026-10-10)

- [relocation] The --diff view truncates long string literals with '....': Erm_fn_ClearLastError's second message shows as "...clear of the last error...." but the retail bytes are "...last error.\n But there is no error !". When only a string relocation stays unresolved, read the literal from the ELF at the lui/addiu address instead of copying the diff text. Separately, a loop that retail guards with one condition test and then enters by jumping to the same test is 'if (cond) { for (; cond; step) ... }'; a plain for/while drops the guard (lines=ok once matched). (2026-10-10)

- [compiler] Scoped pragma sweep (Persona 4 decomp method, reproduced on our compilers). 3.0.1 accepts opt_propagation, opt_lifetimes, opt_common_subs, opt_dead_assignments, opt_dead_code, opt_loop_invariants, opt_strength_reduction(_strict), opt_unroll_loops, peephole, schedule, conditional_move, global_optimizer and optimization_level 0-4. It does NOT accept b210's no_branch_likely, opt_pulloutconstants, opt_rotateloops or opt_repositioncode (unknown pragmas are silently ignored; validate with '#pragma warn_illpragma on'). Sweeping each knob on/off around one function (#pragma push / knob / #pragma pop) over 87 same-size near-misses matched 4: GLI_vSetGlobalAlpha (opt_propagation off), impPS2glPushMatrix (peephole off), CLinkActor::mfn_bExecuteActorInstance (opt_lifetimes off), fn_vPlayNewLipsSynchro (optimization_level 4). Each records its justification next to the pragma. Also confirmed: plain C never gives mula/madda/madd (always mul/mul/adda/madd) or mula/madda/madda/msub on 3.0.1, so those asm macros stay. (2026-10-10)

- [source-layout] Mirror of the .cxx-as-C finding: winmain.c's debug records say Language: C++, and main's 'static int counter = 0' inside the thread-switch loop has a C++ init guard (init$544). '#pragma cplusplus on' at the top plus one extern "C" { } block around the file reproduces it, keeping main/SetPal60/SetPal50 unmangled, and the TU is 3/3. Check the 'Language:' header line whenever a guard appears or disappears unexpectedly. Also, main's float literals passed to variadic sprintf promote to double (13246.513f gives 0x40c9df41a0000000), and the extractor declared function symbols (fn_vEngine, PS2GL_malloc...) as function-pointer variables; taking the address of a real function fixes the relocs. (2026-10-10)

- [compiler] Dead stack-vector copies: retail fn_vTreatCharactersInSector copies hGlobalMatrix translation to an unused local with three lw/sw to sp slots and no address register. MWCC 3.0.1/3.0.3 delete every plain/union/struct/inline/field copy of an unused local; casts through &v keep it but materialize addiu v,sp,N. Declaring it 'volatile unsigned int v[3]' and storing v[i] = ((unsigned int*)&src)[i] reproduces the retail bytes exactly. Separately, moving the initialized local 'unsigned char bSectorChange = 1;' to the first declaration fixed an s0/s1 swap with the parameter, and ordering h_Current3dData, ulCurrentBits, bNoDisplay next gave the retail s2/s3/s4. MWCC never fills a branch delay slot with an asm{} mtc0 from the join block, which retail does (b +4; mtc0 in slot). (2026-10-11)

- [compiler] COP0 mtc0 $24 in branch delay slots: write the asm with the R5900 alias 'mtbpc REG' (e.g. asm { mtbpc zero } or asm { mtbpc toto } with a C variable) instead of 'mtc0 REG, $24'. MWCC assembles both to the same word, but schedules mtbpc like an ordinary instruction: it fills branch delay slots (retail 'b X; mtc0' including copies of a join block's first instruction with the branch retargeted +4) and gets its hazard nop automatically, so .set noreorder and explicit nops are not needed. The mtc0 spelling, __I_mtc0(gpr, cop0reg) (immediates only), GCC-style __asm__, pragmas and 3.0/3.0.3/3.0b38 are never moved into slots. Matched PS2_fn_vEnterCriticalSection with only that respelling; fixes the delay-slot write in FireFx_vProcess and fn_vTreatCharactersInSector. ~24 functions have mtc0 $24 in a delay slot. (2026-10-11)

- [source-layout] Saved-register swaps that survive every declaration-order permutation can come from an intermediate value the original stored in an existing debug local. TBF_pReadLevel stayed EQUIVALENT (Length/Buffer s1/s2 swapped) under all 24 local orders; the permuter matched it with 'Buffer = PLD_szPrependGamedataName(FileName); TBF_File = CD_OpenFileWithNameConversion(Buffer);' instead of the nested call. Likewise fn_ucIntelligenceRulesEngine kept stValue's flag byte in a saved register only when it was assigned to the otherwise-dead ucIndTabAction local (debug info lists no separate flags local). When locals look under-used, try routing call results through them before random permutation. (2026-10-11)
